# F: Recent sweep (arXiv submissions 2026-05-01 to 2026-10-22), LLM agents for software engineering

Method: arXiv API (Atom), ~60 keyword queries sorted by submittedDate, filtered to published >= 2026-05-01, deduplicated by id. 2953 unique entries retrieved; 1741 passed a keyword prefilter on SWE-related terms and were triaged from title+abstract; 524 dropped as irrelevant; 1217 kept. Entries not matching any SWE-related keyword (about 1212) were not triaged. One-line contributions were written from the abstract as returned by the API (some triaged on the first ~1,000 characters only). Tags in brackets: [S] = small open model (<=14B) trained/evaluated as SWE agent; [C1]..[C8] = possible collision with idea 1..8 (see last section).

Counts per theme: scaffold/inference 153, training/RL/distillation 135, graphs/retrieval/localization 64, benchmarks/evaluation 329, context/memory/budget 115, small-models/efficiency 16, other-relevant 405, irrelevant (dropped) 524.

## Possible collisions

Flags assigned during triage from abstracts; strength is marked in the review notes below the tables where known. Always verify by reading the paper.


### Idea 1. Code graphs / graph tools vs grep; marginal information of a code graph

| arXiv id | date | title | why flagged |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2605.16352v1 | 2026-05-08 | LARGER: Lexically Anchored Repository Graph Exploration and Retrieval | LARGER: lexically anchored repository graph retrieval integrated into the agent's existing lexical search loop, adding confidence-filtered structural neighborhoods for localization. |
| 2606.14061v4 | 2026-06-12 | LLM Agents Can See Code Repositories | First study of visual repository representations for agents on issue resolution: vision-only degrades accuracy, but visual structure graphs added to text cut input tokens by up to 26%. |
| 2607.09691v1 | 2026-06-19 | What Context Does a Coding Agent Actually Need to Act? | With localization fixed by oracle on SWE-bench Verified, the edited code itself carries the signal: NL summaries answer 4/45 vs 27/45 behavioral questions for source, and surrounding-file context hardly matters. |
| 2606.22417v1 | 2026-06-21 | Code Isn't Memory: A Structural Codebase Index Inside a Coding Agent | Structural codebase index in a fixed Opus 4.7 harness: large localization gain, significant resolve gain on SWE-PolyBench Verified and SWE-bench Pro, no regression versus agentic grep, lower cost per solve. |
| 2606.26979v2 | 2026-06-25 | How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring | Injecting lightweight call/inheritance static structure as comments into a Codex agent gives modest localization gains (+2.2 pp Func@5) and more disciplined, reproducible navigation than keyword search. |
| 2608.13568v1 | 2026-06-29 | Does a Language Server Save Tokens for Coding Agents? A Measurement Methodology and Preliminary Study | Measures whether a language server saves tokens for coding agents versus grep; on symbol-named localization LSP costs +6% to +118% tokens, and gives precision but no token savings on references. |

| 2607.27249v1 | 2026-07-28 | OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair | OwlPath encodes code into an OWL2 ontology over CodeGraph so SPARQL queries fetch multi-hop structural dependencies (callers, subclasses) compactly for LLM bug repair. |
